## ⊕² §0.S · THE ONE MINTED DESIGN RULING ✦² — the grasp-release frost-thinning cure (the fold charter licenses exactly one; ruled from the measured table — the CAPTURE ADDENDUM — never averaged; the cause reproduced arithmetically at S-7 AND re-reproduced at this seat, A-5)

**THE SEQUENCED-FLOOR CROSS-COVER with the decoupled veil and a bounded over-frost gate. Lands at #22's rung system as the `glass/grasp` partial's release law. Both arms converged on clauses 1-2 independently; clause 3 is the Fable arm's forcing falsifier; clause 4's bound-as-gate is the Opus arm's; the adjudicator owns this merge.**

1. **Decouple the veil.** The ink veil leaves both rungs and lives on ONE always-opaque, never-filtering layer whose ink lerps between the grasp ink (`.18` light / `.20` dark) and the rest ink (`.30` light / `.34` dark) across the release envelope — monotone by construction, endpoints exact at every age. This removes the measured stacking at its cause: S-7/A-5 show the blocked cure's α 0.472/0.426 IS `1−(1−α_rest)(1−α_grasp)` to three decimals, which is only reachable when two layers each carry a veil.
2. **Never both below one.** The two rungs become **filter-only** carriers. At release the rest rung steps to opacity **1 first**; the grasp rung then fades 1→0 over the ~180 ms envelope. The un-frosted fraction `(1−o_g)(1−o_r) ≡ 0` at every frame — against the measured **25% raw substrate at o=0.5** under the complementary crossfade (σ-kept 12.7% → 24.1% mid-release). Entry is unchanged: a true 0 ms discrete step, no crossfade, nothing to cover.
3. **Why nothing weaker suffices — the forcing falsifier.** Any floor α<1 leaves a nonzero leak (`(1−α)²` at the crossing); and applying the ≡0 sequenced floor to rungs that still CARRY veil ink stacks the veils transiently — `1−(1−.30)(1−.18) = .426` mid-release, a dip DARKER than rest, the exit-overshoot class MOTION-CANON §7 forbids. The veil decoupling in clause 1 is therefore **forced, not stylistic**: clauses 1 and 2 stand or fall together.
4. **The over-frost is bounded, and the bound is the gate.** During the overlap both filters apply; stacked Gaussians compose in quadrature, so the effective radius peaks at `√(16² + 26²) = 30.5px` — **1.17× the grasp endpoint**, decaying monotonically to 16px. Legal under the monotone-dilution law (over-frost never thins; the direction matches the grasp move 16→26). **Gate clause: peak effective backdrop radius ≤ 1.25× the grasp rung's radius**, measured on the release trace — a checkable ceiling rather than a promise.
5. **Rest cost stays zero.** Both rungs mount **engage-only**, so the measured **+1.07 ms/frame at 32 plates (≈33 µs per resting rung)** never accrues at rest; #69 owns the many-surface arithmetic, and its row is now sourced.
6. **Falsifiers, on record and armed.** Any release frame with `(1−o_g)(1−o_r) > 0` → clause 2 failed. Peak effective radius > 1.25× → clause 4 failed and the sequencing inverts (grasp holds, rest fades in) at the cost of an under-frost dip — the strictly worse branch, recorded so it is one word away. A decoupled-veil α outside the monotone band `[.18,.30]`/`[.20,.34]` at any sampled f → clause 1 failed. The 60fps-during-drag arm stays armed at **#67's device cell**; the cure's own release trace is owed at the next sequenced capture (**owner: #22's land seat; trigger: the first grasp π**). **Owner-reversible in one word** to the LAW-9 confinement branch — dilution stays confined to the album-morph timeline (#47 W7), where it is already owned.

**Provenance:** both fold arms reached clauses 1-2 independently (the Fable arm from the addendum's table + the dead draft's prior construction, disclosed; the Opus arm from the stacking identity, reproduced); the Opus arm read the Fable text before ruling and said so. This seat re-derived the identity and the quadrature bound from the addendum's raw cells before reading either arm's §2 conclusions against them (A-5). Convergence is corroboration; this merge is the ruling of record.

# §C · CONSTELLATION READINESS (fresh-census-at-ship stays law)

**The consumer definition is RULED** (RATIFICATION §1.1): a distinct importing file outside the module's directory, over runtime import edges (TS/JS + CSS `@import`), in three labeled classes—library · demo · **cross-repo**. **⊕² THE UNIVERSE IS GENERATED, NOT REMEMBERED (S-10).** The eight names below are a per-repo ROW INDEX, never the universe: the boundary is generated from package manifests declaring `@mkbabb/glass-ui` plus registered alias/workspace consumers — **15 closed-universe roots · 6 typed subpaths · 3 operational mirrors · 1 negative control** (`docs/tranches/BJ/audits/2026-07-28-consumer-constellation/CONSUMER-LEDGER.{md,json}`). ⊘ re-verified this seat: **words** (97 refs — carousel, hover-card ×13, toast ×11, button ×29), **muster** (181 — instrument-chassis, toggle-chip, configurator ×7, motion ×5), **slides-k** (73), **bbnf-lang** (31), latex-paper (11, text-only), parse-that (5, text-only), **oscilloscope 0** (the negative control holds). Standing law: **a zero direct-import count does not prove vacuity** (transitive and manifest edges remain in the universe), **a non-zero count does not prove a sector deserves to survive** (Ruling 1), **absence from a relay table is not a zero-consumer assertion**, and ✦³ **muster is a PROTOTYPE repo — its consumer counts never bind a disposition** (round 2 item 9, SL-3; muster rows survive as relay/migration evidence only). The `@import` edge ADDS a member, never removes one. No census ships without class + regex. **G-RELAY enforces it.** Existence ⇒ relay, never ⇒ KEEP (Ruling 1); publish, not commit, closes consumer rows (L2).
